{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Full bit-width PPL profiler for quant-only MCKP.\\nProfiles each KV head at {2, 4, 8, 16}-bit quantization.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport math\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\nfrom eval_ppl import patch_attention, restore_attention\\n\\n\\nclass FullBitwidthProfiler:\\n    def __init__(self, model_path, calibration_data, device='cuda:0',\\n                 num_samples=16, seq_len=512):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.seq_len = seq_len\\n        self.num_samples = num_samples\\n\\n        print(f\\\"Loading model {model_path}...\\\")\\n        self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path)\\n        self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n            model_path, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\"\\n        )\\n        self.model = self.model.to(device)\\n        self.model.eval()\\n\\n        config = self.model.config\\n        self.num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\\n        self.num_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads\\n\\n        self.input_ids = self._load_calibration_data(calibration_data)\\n        total_heads = self.num_layers * self.num_kv_heads\\n        print(f\\\"Config: {self.num_layers} layers, {self.num_kv_heads} KV heads/layer, \\\"\\n              f\\\"{total_heads} heads total\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"Calibration: {self.input_ids.shape[0]} seqs x {self.input_ids.shape[1]} tokens\\\")\\n\\n        self._baseline_ppl = None\\n\\n    def _load_calibration_data(self, path):\\n        with open(path) as f:\\n            samples = json.load(f)\\n        all_ids = []\\n        for s in samples:\\n            tokens = self.tokenizer.encode(s[\\\"text\\\"], add_special_tokens=False)\\n            if len(tokens) >= self.seq_len:\\n                all_ids.append(tokens[:self.seq_len])\\n            if len(all_ids) >= self.num_samples:\\n                break\\n        if not all_ids:\\n            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"No calibration seqs with >= {self.seq_len} tokens\\\")\\n        return torch.tensor(all_ids, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\\n\\n    def _compute_ppl(self):\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = self.model(\\n                self.input_ids, use_cache=False,\\n                cache_position=torch.arange(self.seq_len, device=self.device)\\n            )\\n        shift_logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1].contiguous()\\n        shift_labels = self.input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\\n        loss = F.cross_entropy(\\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.shape[-1]),\\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\\n            reduction=\\\"sum\\\"\\n        )\\n        total_tokens = shift_labels.numel()\\n        return math.exp(loss.item() / total_tokens)\\n\\n    def compute_baseline_ppl(self):\\n        if self._baseline_ppl is None:\\n            print(\\\"Computing baseline PPL...\\\")\\n            self._baseline_ppl = self._compute_ppl()\\n            print(f\\\"Baseline PPL: {self._baseline_ppl:.4f}\\\")\\n        return self._baseline_ppl\\n\\n    def profile_single_head(self, layer, head, bits):\\n        _ = self.compute_baseline_ppl()\\n        if bits >= 16:\\n            return 0.0\\n        allocations = {f\\\"{layer},{head}\\\": [\\\"quant\\\", bits]}\\n        orig_fwds = patch_attention(self.model, allocations)\\n        ppl = self._compute_ppl()\\n        restore_attention(self.model, orig_fwds)\\n        return ppl - self._baseline_ppl\\n\\n    def profile_all(self, bit_widths=(2, 4, 8)):\\n        _ = self.compute_baseline_ppl()\\n        total_heads = self.num_layers * self.num_kv_heads\\n        total_evals = total_heads * len(bit_widths)\\n        done = 0\\n\\n        profiles = {}\\n        for l in range(self.num_layers):\\n            profiles[str(l)] = {}\\n            for h in range(self.num_kv_heads):\\n                head_profile = {\\\"quantization\\\": {}}\\n                for bits in bit_widths:\\n                    delta = self.profile_single_head(l, h, bits)\\n                    head_profile[\\\"quantization\\\"][str(bits)] = delta\\n                    done += 1\\n                    print(f\\\"[{done}/{total_evals}] L{l}H{h} @{bits}bit: delta={delta:.6f}\\\")\\n                profiles[str(l)][str(h)] = head_profile\\n\\n        return profiles\\n\", \"stderr\":       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Full bit-width PPL profiler for quant-only MCKP.\\nProfiles each KV head at {2, 4, 8, 16}-bit quantization.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport math\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\nfrom eval_ppl import patch_attention, restore_attention\\n\\n\\nclass FullBitwidthProfiler:\\n    def __init__(self, model_path, calibration_data, device='cuda:0',\\n                 num_samples=16, seq_len=512):\\n        self.device = device\\n        self.seq_len = seq_len\\n        self.num_samples = num_samples\\n\\n        print(f\\\"Loading model {model_path}...\\\")\\n        self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path)\\n        self.model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n            model_path, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\"\\n        )\\n        self.model = self.model.to(device)\\n        self.model.eval()\\n\\n        config = self.model.config\\n        self.num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\\n        self.num_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads\\n\\n        self.input_ids = self._load_calibration_data(calibration_data)\\n        total_heads = self.num_layers * self.num_kv_heads\\n        print(f\\\"Config: {self.num_layers} layers, {self.num_kv_heads} KV heads/layer, \\\"\\n              f\\\"{total_heads} heads total\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"Calibration: {self.input_ids.shape[0]} seqs x {self.input_ids.shape[1]} tokens\\\")\\n\\n        self._baseline_ppl = None\\n\\n    def _load_calibration_data(self, path):\\n        with open(path) as f:\\n            samples = json.load(f)\\n        all_ids = []\\n        for s in samples:\\n            tokens = self.tokenizer.encode(s[\\\"text\\\"], add_special_tokens=False)\\n            if len(tokens) >= self.seq_len:\\n                all_ids.append(tokens[:self.seq_len])\\n            if len(all_ids) >= self.num_samples:\\n                break\\n        if not all_ids:\\n            raise RuntimeError(f\\\"No calibration seqs with >= {self.seq_len} tokens\\\")\\n        return torch.tensor(all_ids, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\\n\\n    def _compute_ppl(self):\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = self.model(\\n                self.input_ids, use_cache=False,\\n                cache_position=torch.arange(self.seq_len, device=self.device)\\n            )\\n        shift_logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1].contiguous()\\n        shift_labels = self.input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\\n        loss = F.cross_entropy(\\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.shape[-1]),\\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\\n            reduction=\\\"sum\\\"\\n        )\\n        total_tokens = shift_labels.numel()\\n        return math.exp(loss.item() / total_tokens)\\n\\n    def compute_baseline_ppl(self):\\n        if self._baseline_ppl is None:\\n            print(\\\"Computing baseline PPL...\\\")\\n            self._baseline_ppl = self._compute_ppl()\\n            print(f\\\"Baseline PPL: {self._baseline_ppl:.4f}\\\")\\n        return self._baseline_ppl\\n\\n    def profile_single_head(self, layer, head, bits):\\n        _ = self.compute_baseline_ppl()\\n        if bits >= 16:\\n            return 0.0\\n        allocations = {f\\\"{layer},{head}\\\": [\\\"quant\\\", bits]}\\n        orig_fwds = patch_attention(self.model, allocations)\\n        ppl = self._compute_ppl()\\n        restore_attention(self.model, orig_fwds)\\n        return ppl - self._baseline_ppl\\n\\n    def profile_all(self, bit_widths=(2, 4, 8)):\\n        _ = self.compute_baseline_ppl()\\n        total_heads = self.num_layers * self.num_kv_heads\\n        total_evals = total_heads * len(bit_widths)\\n        done = 0\\n\\n        profiles = {}\\n        for l in range(self.num_layers):\\n            profiles[str(l)] = {}\\n            for h in range(self.num_kv_heads):\\n                head_profile = {\\\"quantization\\\": {}}\\n                for bits in bit_widths:\\n                    delta = self.profile_single_head(l, h, bits)\\n                    head_profile[\\\"quantization\\\"][str(bits)] = delta\\n                    done += 1\\n                    print(f\\\"[{done}/{total_evals}] L{l}H{h} @{bits}bit: delta={delta:.6f}\\\")\\n                profiles[str(l)][str(h)] = head_profile\\n\\n        return profiles\\n\", \"stderr\": 1e-12:\\n            pareto.append(a)\\n\\n    # Sort by memory descending for greedy downgrade\\n    pareto.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])\\n    return pareto\\n\\n\\ndef solve_mckp(profiles, budget, mode=\\\"full\\\"):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Solve MCKP for KV cache allocation via greedy downgrade.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        profiles: {layer: {head: {eviction: {ratio: mse}, quantization: {bits: mse}}}}\\n        budget: target average memory ratio per head (e.g. 0.25 for 4x compression)\\n        mode: \\\"full\\\" (eviction + quantization) or \\\"quant_only\\\"\\n    Returns:\\n        dict with allocation, total_distortion, actual_memory_ratio\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    heads = []\\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n            l, h = int(l_str), int(h_str)\\n            pareto = _build_pareto(profiles[l_str][h_str], mode)\\n            heads.append((l, h, pareto))\\n\\n    n_heads = len(heads)\\n    target = budget * n_heads\\n\\n    # Start all at most expensive (index 0 = full_16bit)\\n    current = {(l, h): 0 for l, h, _ in heads}\\n    total_memory = sum(a[0][0] for _, _, a in heads)\\n\\n    # Greedy downgrade: pick lowest efficiency (distortion_increase / memory_saved)\\n    while total_memory > target + 1e-9:\\n        best_eff = float(\\\"inf\\\")\\n        best_key = None\\n        best_idx = None\\n\\n        for l, h, actions in heads:\\n            ci = current[(l, h)]\\n            if ci + 1 >= len(actions):\\n                continue\\n            curr = actions[ci]\\n            for ni in range(ci + 1, len(actions)):\\n                nxt = actions[ni]\\n                mem_saved = curr[0] - {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"[\\n  {\\n    \\\"text\\\": \\\"Senj\\\\u014d no Valkyria 3 : Unrecorded Chronicles ( Japanese : \\\\u6226\\\\u5834\\\\u306e\\\\u30f4\\\\u30a1\\\\u30eb\\\\u30ad\\\\u30e5\\\\u30ea\\\\u30a23 , lit . Valkyria of the Battlefield 3 ) , commonly referred to as Valkyria Chronicles III outside Japan , is a tactical role @-@ playing video game developed by Sega and Media.Vision for the PlayStation Portable . Released in January 2011 in Japan , it is the third game in the Valkyria series . Employing the same fusion of tactical and real @-@ time gameplay as its predecessors , the story runs parallel to the first game and follows the \\\\\\\" Nameless \\\\\\\" , a penal military unit serving the nation of Gallia during the Second Europan War who perform secret black operations and are pitted against the Imperial unit \\\\\\\" Calamaty Raven \\\\\\\" . The game began development in 2010 , carrying over a large portion of the work done on Valkyria Chronicles II . While it retained the standard features of the series , it also underwent multiple adjustments , such as making the game more forgiving for series newcomers . Character designer Raita Honjou and composer Hitoshi Sakimoto both returned from previous entries , along with Valkyria Chronicles II director Takeshi Ozawa . A large team of writers handled the script . The game 's opening theme was sung by May 'n . It met with positive sales in Japan , and was praised by both Japanese and western critics . After release , it received downloadable content , along with an expanded edition in November of that year . It was also adapted into manga and an original video animation series . Due to low sales of Valkyria Chronicles II , Valkyria Chronicles III was not localized , but a fan translation compatible with the game 's expanded edition was released in 2014 . Media.Vision would return to the franchise with the development of Valkyria : Azure Revolution for the PlayStation 4 . As with previous Valkyira Chronicles games , Valkyria Chronicles III is a tactical role @-@ playing game where players take control of a military unit and take part in missions against enemy forces . Stories are told through comic book @-@ like panels with animated character portraits , with characters speaking partially through voiced speech bubbles and partially through unvoiced text . The player progresses through a series of linear missions , gradually unlocked as maps that can be freely scanned through and replayed as they are unlocked . The route to each story location on the map varies depending on an individual player 's approach : when one option is selected , the other is sealed off to the player . Outside missions , the player characters rest in a camp , where units can be customized and character growth occurs . Alongside the main story missions are character @-@ specific sub missions relating to different squad members . After the game 's completion , additional episodes are unlocked , some of them having a higher difficulty than those found in the rest of the game . There are also love simulation elements related to the game 's two main heroines , although they take a very minor role . The game 's battle system , the BliTZ system , is carried over directly from Valkyira Chronicles . During missions , players select each unit using a top @-@ down perspective of the battlefield map : once a character is selected , the player moves the character around the battlefield in third @-@ person . A character can only act once per @-@ turn , but characters can be granted multiple turns at the expense of other characters ' turns . Each character has a field and distance of movement limited by their Action Gauge . Up to nine characters can be assigned to a single mission . During gameplay , characters will call out if something happens to them , such as their health points ( HP ) getting low or being knocked out by enemy attacks . Each character has specific \\\\\\\" Potentials \\\\\\\" , skills unique to each character . They are divided into \\\\\\\" Personal Potential \\\\\\\" , which are innate skills that remain unaltered unless otherwise dictated by the story and can either help or impede a character , and \\\\\\\" Battle Potentials \\\\\\\" , which are grown throughout the game and always grant boons to a character . To learn Battle Potentials , each character has a unique \\\\\\\" Masters Table \\\\\\\" , a grid @-@ based skill table that can be used to acquire and link different skills . Characters also have Special Abilities that grant them temporary boosts on the battlefield : Kurt can activate \\\\\\\" Direct Command \\\\\\\" and move around the battlefield without depleting his Action Point gauge , the character Reila can shift into her \\\\\\\" Valkyria Form \\\\\\\" and become invincible , while Imca can target multiple enemy units with her heavy weapon . Troops are divided into five classes : Scouts , Shocktroopers , Engineers , Lancers and Armored Soldier . Troopers can switch classes by changing their assigned weapon . Changing class does not greatly affect the stats gained while in a previous class . With victory in battle , experience points are awarded to the squad , which are distributed into five different attributes shared by the entire squad , a feature differing from early games ' method of distributing to different unit types .\\\"\\n  },\\n  {\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.52}}"}

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"MCKP solver for KV cache allocation.\\nInput: distortion profiles + memory budget\\nOutput: per-head allocation decisions\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\n\\n\\ndef _build_pareto(head_data, mode):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build Pareto-optimal action set for a single head.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    actions = [(1.0, 0.0, \\\"full\\\", 16)]\\n\\n    if mode == \\\"full\\\":\\n        for r_str, mse in head_data.get(\\\"eviction\\\", {}).items():\\n            r = float(r_str)\\n            actions.append((r, mse, \\\"evict\\\", r))\\n\\n    for b_str, mse in head_data.get(\\\"quantization\\\", {}).items():\\n        b = int(float(b_str))\\n        actions.append((b / 16.0, mse, \\\"quant\\\", b))\\n\\n    # Deduplicate by memory: keep lowest distortion per memory level\\n    by_mem = {}\\n    for a in actions:\\n        mem = round(a[0], 6)\\n        if mem not in by_mem or a[1] < by_mem[mem][1]:\\n            by_mem[mem] = a\\n\\n    # Sort by memory ascending, build Pareto front\\n    sorted_actions = sorted(by_mem.values(), key=lambda x: x[0])\\n    pareto = [sorted_actions[0]]\\n    for a in sorted_actions[1:]:\\n        if a[1] < pareto[-1][1] - 1e-12:\\n            pareto.append(a)\\n\\n    # Sort by memory descending for greedy downgrade\\n    pareto.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])\\n    return pareto\\n\\n\\ndef solve_mckp(profiles, budget, mode=\\\"full\\\"):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Solve MCKP for KV cache allocation via greedy downgrade.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        profiles: {layer: {head: {eviction: {ratio: mse}, quantization: {bits: mse}}}}\\n        budget: target average memory ratio per head (e.g. 0.25 for 4x compression)\\n        mode: \\\"full\\\" (eviction + quantization) or \\\"quant_only\\\"\\n    Returns:\\n        dict with allocation, total_distortion, actual_memory_ratio\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    heads = []\\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n            l, h = int(l_str), int(h_str)\\n            pareto = _build_pareto(profiles[l_str][h_str], mode)\\n            heads.append((l, h, pareto))\\n\\n    n_heads = len(heads)\\n    target = budget * n_heads\\n\\n    # Start all at most expensive (index 0 = full_16bit)\\n    current = {(l, h): 0 for l, h, _ in heads}\\n    total_memory = sum(a[0][0] for _, _, a in heads)\\n\\n    # Greedy downgrade: pick lowest efficiency (distortion_increase / memory_saved)\\n    while total_memory > target + 1e-9:\\n        best_eff = float(\\\"inf\\\")\\n        best_key = None\\n        best_idx = None\\n\\n        for l, h, actions in heads:\\n            ci = current[(l, h)]\\n            if ci + 1 >= len(actions):\\n                continue\\n            curr = actions[ci]\\n            for ni in range(ci + 1, len(actions)):\\n                nxt = actions[ni]\\n                mem_saved = curr[0] - nxt[0]\\n                if mem_saved < 1e-12:\\n                    continue\\n                eff = (nxt[1] - curr[1]) / mem_saved\\n                if eff < best_eff:\\n                    best_eff = eff\\n                    best_key = (l, h)\\n                    best_idx = ni\\n\\n        if best_key is None:\\n            break\\n\\n        l, h = best_key\\n        for ll, hh, actions in heads:\\n            if ll == l and hh == h:\\n                old = actions[current[best_key]]\\n                new = actions[best_idx]\\n                total_memory -= (old[0] - new[0])\\n                current[best_key] = best_idx\\n                break\\n\\n    # Format output\\n    allocation = {}\\n    total_dist = 0.0\\n    actual_mem = 0.0\\n    for l, h, actions in heads:\\n        a = actions[current[(l, h)]]\\n        allocation[f\\\"{l},{h}\\\"] = [a[2], a[3]]\\n        total_dist += a[1]\\n        actual_mem += a[0]\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"allocation\\\": allocation,\\n        \\\"total_distortion\\\": total_dist,\\n        \\\"actual_memory_ratio\\\": actual_mem / n_heads,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef uniform_quantization_baseline(profiles, bits=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"All heads use same quantization bit-width.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    allocation = {}\\n    total_dist = 0.0\\n    n_heads = 0\\n    mem_ratio = bits / 16.0\\n\\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n \\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 945 字符] ...\\n\\nal memory ratio: {result['actual_memory_ratio']:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Total distortion: {result['total_distortion']:.6f}\\\")\\n\\n    types = {}\\n    for key, (atype, param) in result[\\\"allocation\\\"].items():\\n        types[atype] = types.get(atype, 0) + 1\\n    print(f\\\"Action distribution: {types}\\\")\\n\\n    with open(args.output, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(result, f, indent=2)\\n    print(f\\\"Saved to {args.output}\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\\n\\ndef _build_actions_no_pareto(head_data, mode, exclude_bits=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build full action set without Pareto filtering.\\n    Needed for constrained solver: Pareto filtering removes dominated actions,\\n    but cardinality constraints can make the Pareto-optimal choice infeasible.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    actions = [(1.0, 0.0, \\\"full\\\", 16)]\\n\\n    if mode == \\\"full\\\":\\n        for r_str, mse in head_data.get(\\\"eviction\\\", {}).items():\\n            r = float(r_str)\\n            actions.append((r, mse, \\\"evict\\\", r))\\n\\n    for b_str, mse in head_data.get(\\\"quantization\\\", {}).items():\\n        b = int(float(b_str))\\n        if exclude_bits and b in exclude_bits:\\n            continue\\n        actions.append((b / 16.0, mse, \\\"quant\\\", b))\\n\\n    by_mem = {}\\n    for a in actions:\\n        mem = round(a[0], 6)\\n        if mem not in by_mem or a[1] < by_mem[mem][1]:\\n            by_mem[mem] = a\\n\\n    sorted_actions = sorted(by_mem.values(), key=lambda x: -x[0])\\n    return sorted_actions\\n\\n\\ndef solve_mckp_constrained(profiles, budget, mode=\\\"quant_only\\\",\\n                           max_2bit=8, l0_exclude_2bit=True):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"MCKP with cardinality constraints (D009).\\n    - max_2bit: at most K heads at 2-bit (K=8, manual experiment safe zone)\\n    - l0_exclude_2bit: Layer 0 heads excluded from 2-bit (cliff effect)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    heads = []\\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n            l, h = int(l_str), int(h_str)\\n            exclude = {2} if (l0_exclude_2bit and l == 0) else set()\\n            actions = _build_actions_no_pareto(profiles[l_str][h_str], mode, exclude)\\n            heads.append((l, h, actions))\\n\\n    n_heads = len(heads)\\n    target = budget * n_heads\\n    current = {(l, h): 0 for l, h, _ in heads}\\n    total_memory = sum(a[0][0] for _, _, a in heads)\\n    n_2bit = 0\\n\\n    while total_memory > target + 1e-9:\\n        best_eff = float(\\\"inf\\\")\\n        best_saved = 0.0\\n        best_key = None\\n        best_idx = None\\n\\n        for l, h, actions in heads:\\n            ci = current[(l, h)]\\n            if ci + 1 >= len(actions):\\n                continue\\n            curr = actions[ci]\\n            for ni in range(ci + 1, len(actions)):\\n                nxt = actions[ni]\\n                mem_saved = curr[0] - nxt[0]\\n                if mem_saved < 1e-12:\\n                    continue\\n                # D009: cardinality constraint on 2-bit\\n                is_2bit = (nxt[2] == \\\"quant\\\" and nxt[3] == 2)\\n                if is_2bit and max_2bit is not None and n_2bit >= max_2bit:\\n                    continue\\n                eff = (nxt[1] - curr[1]) / mem_saved\\n                # Prefer lower eff; break ties by larger mem_saved for faster convergence\\n                if eff < best_eff - 1e-12 or (abs(eff - best_eff) < 1e-12 and mem_saved > best_saved):\\n                    best_eff = eff\\n                    best_saved = mem_saved\\n                    best_key = (l, h)\\n                    best_idx = ni\\n\\n        if best_key is None:\\n            break\\n\\n        l, h = best_key\\n        for ll, hh, actions in heads:\\n            if ll == l and hh == h:\\n                old = actions[current[best_key]]\\n                new = actions[best_idx]\\n                total_memory -= (old[0] - new[0])\\n                was_2bit = (old[2] == \\\"quant\\\" and old[3] == 2)\\n                is_now_2bit = (new[2] == \\\"quant\\\" and new[3] == 2)\\n                if is_now_2bit and not was_2bit:\\n                    n_2bit += 1\\n                elif was_2bit and not is_now_2bit:\\n                    n_2bit -= 1\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"MCKP solver for KV cache allocation.\\nInput: distortion profiles + memory budget\\nOutput: per-head allocation decisions\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\n\\n\\ndef _build_pareto(head_data, mode):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build Pareto-optimal action set for a single head.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    actions = [(1.0, 0.0, \\\"full\\\", 16)]\\n\\n    if mode == \\\"full\\\":\\n        for r_str, mse in head_data.get(\\\"eviction\\\", {}).items():\\n            r = float(r_str)\\n            actions.append((r, mse, \\\"evict\\\", r))\\n\\n    for b_str, mse in head_data.get(\\\"quantization\\\", {}).items():\\n        b = int(float(b_str))\\n        actions.append((b / 16.0, mse, \\\"quant\\\", b))\\n\\n    # Deduplicate by memory: keep lowest distortion per memory level\\n    by_mem = {}\\n    for a in actions:\\n        mem = round(a[0], 6)\\n        if mem not in by_mem or a[1] < by_mem[mem][1]:\\n            by_mem[mem] = a\\n\\n    # Sort by memory ascending, build Pareto front\\n    sorted_actions = sorted(by_mem.values(), key=lambda x: x[0])\\n    pareto = [sorted_actions[0]]\\n    for a in sorted_actions[1:]:\\n        if a[1] < pareto[-1][1] - 1e-12:\\n            pareto.append(a)\\n\\n    # Sort by memory descending for greedy downgrade\\n    pareto.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])\\n    return pareto\\n\\n\\ndef solve_mckp(profiles, budget, mode=\\\"full\\\"):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Solve MCKP for KV cache allocation via greedy downgrade.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        profiles: {layer: {head: {eviction: {ratio: mse}, quantization: {bits: mse}}}}\\n        budget: target average memory ratio per head (e.g. 0.25 for 4x compression)\\n        mode: \\\"full\\\" (eviction + quantization) or \\\"quant_only\\\"\\n    Returns:\\n        dict with allocation, total_distortion, actual_memory_ratio\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    heads = []\\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n            l, h = int(l_str), int(h_str)\\n            pareto = _build_pareto(profiles[l_str][h_str], mode)\\n            heads.append((l, h, pareto))\\n\\n    n_heads = len(heads)\\n    target = budget * n_heads\\n\\n    # Start all at most expensive (index 0 = full_16bit)\\n    current = {(l, h): 0 for l, h, _ in heads}\\n    total_memory = sum(a[0][0] for _, _, a in heads)\\n\\n    # Greedy downgrade: pick lowest efficiency (distortion_increase / memory_saved)\\n    while total_memory > target + 1e-9:\\n        best_eff = float(\\\"inf\\\")\\n        best_key = None\\n        best_idx = None\\n\\n        for l, h, actions in heads:\\n            ci = current[(l, h)]\\n            if ci + 1 >= len(actions):\\n                continue\\n            curr = actions[ci]\\n            for ni in range(ci + 1, len(actions)):\\n                nxt = actions[ni]\\n                mem_saved = curr[0] - nxt[0]\\n                if mem_saved < 1e-12:\\n                    continue\\n                eff = (nxt[1] - curr[1]) / mem_saved\\n                if eff < best_eff:\\n                    best_eff = eff\\n                    best_key = (l, h)\\n                    best_idx = ni\\n\\n        if best_key is None:\\n            break\\n\\n        l, h = best_key\\n        for ll, hh, actions in heads:\\n            if ll == l and hh == h:\\n                old = actions[current[best_key]]\\n                new = actions[best_idx]\\n                total_memory -= (old[0] - new[0])\\n                current[best_key] = best_idx\\n                break\\n\\n    # Format output\\n    allocation = {}\\n    total_dist = 0.0\\n    actual_mem = 0.0\\n    for l, h, actions in heads:\\n        a = actions[current[(l, h)]]\\n        allocation[f\\\"{l},{h}\\\"] = [a[2], a[3]]\\n        total_dist += a[1]\\n        actual_mem += a[0]\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"allocation\\\": allocation,\\n        \\\"total_distortion\\\": total_dist,\\n        \\\"actual_memory_ratio\\\": actual_mem / n_heads,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef uniform_quantization_baseline(profiles, bits=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"All heads use same quantization bit-width.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    allocation = {}\\n    total_dist = 0.0\\n    n_heads = 0\\n    mem_ratio = bits / 16.0\\n\\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n \\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 945 字符] ...\\n\\nal memory ratio: {result['actual_memory_ratio']:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Total distortion: {result['total_distortion']:.6f}\\\")\\n\\n    types = {}\\n    for key, (atype, param) in result[\\\"allocation\\\"].items():\\n        types[atype] = types.get(atype, 0) + 1\\n    print(f\\\"Action distribution: {types}\\\")\\n\\n    with open(args.output, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(result, f, indent=2)\\n    print(f\\\"Saved to {args.output}\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\\n\\ndef _build_actions_no_pareto(head_data, mode, exclude_bits=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build full action set without Pareto filtering.\\n    Needed for constrained solver: Pareto filtering removes dominated actions,\\n    but cardinality constraints can make the Pareto-optimal choice infeasible.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    actions = [(1.0, 0.0, \\\"full\\\", 16)]\\n\\n    if mode == \\\"full\\\":\\n        for r_str, mse in head_data.get(\\\"eviction\\\", {}).items():\\n            r = float(r_str)\\n            actions.append((r, mse, \\\"evict\\\", r))\\n\\n    for b_str, mse in head_data.get(\\\"quantization\\\", {}).items():\\n        b = int(float(b_str))\\n        if exclude_bits and b in exclude_bits:\\n            continue\\n        actions.append((b / 16.0, mse, \\\"quant\\\", b))\\n\\n    by_mem = {}\\n    for a in actions:\\n        mem = round(a[0], 6)\\n        if mem not in by_mem or a[1] < by_mem[mem][1]:\\n            by_mem[mem] = a\\n\\n    sorted_actions = sorted(by_mem.values(), key=lambda x: -x[0])\\n    return sorted_actions\\n\\n\\ndef solve_mckp_constrained(profiles, budget, mode=\\\"quant_only\\\",\\n                           max_2bit=8, l0_exclude_2bit=True):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"MCKP with cardinality constraints (D009).\\n    - max_2bit: at most K heads at 2-bit (K=8, manual experiment safe zone)\\n    - l0_exclude_2bit: Layer 0 heads excluded from 2-bit (cliff effect)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    heads = []\\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=lambda x: int(x)):\\n            l, h = int(l_str), int(h_str)\\n            exclude = {2} if (l0_exclude_2bit and l == 0) else set()\\n            actions = _build_actions_no_pareto(profiles[l_str][h_str], mode, exclude)\\n            heads.append((l, h, actions))\\n\\n    n_heads = len(heads)\\n    target = budget * n_heads\\n    current = {(l, h): 0 for l, h, _ in heads}\\n    total_memory = sum(a[0][0] for _, _, a in heads)\\n    n_2bit = 0\\n\\n    while total_memory > target + 1e-9:\\n        best_eff = float(\\\"inf\\\")\\n        best_saved = 0.0\\n        best_key = None\\n        best_idx = None\\n\\n        for l, h, actions in heads:\\n            ci = current[(l, h)]\\n            if ci + 1 >= len(actions):\\n                continue\\n            curr = actions[ci]\\n            for ni in range(ci + 1, len(actions)):\\n                nxt = actions[ni]\\n                mem_saved = curr[0] - nxt[0]\\n                if mem_saved < 1e-12:\\n                    continue\\n                # D009: cardinality constraint on 2-bit\\n                is_2bit = (nxt[2] == \\\"quant\\\" and nxt[3] == 2)\\n                if is_2bit and max_2bit is not None and n_2bit >= max_2bit:\\n                    continue\\n                eff = (nxt[1] - curr[1]) / mem_saved\\n                # Prefer lower eff; break ties by larger mem_saved for faster convergence\\n                if eff < best_eff - 1e-12 or (abs(eff - best_eff) < 1e-12 and mem_saved > best_saved):\\n                    best_eff = eff\\n                    best_saved = mem_saved\\n                    best_key = (l, h)\\n                    best_idx = ni\\n\\n        if best_key is None:\\n            break\\n\\n        l, h = best_key\\n        for ll, hh, actions in heads:\\n            if ll == l and hh == h:\\n                old = actions[current[best_key]]\\n                new = actions[best_idx]\\n                total_memory -= (old[0] - new[0])\\n                was_2bit = (old[2] == \\\"quant\\\" and old[3] == 2)\\n                is_now_2bit = (new[2] == \\\"quant\\\" and new[3] == 2)\\n                if is_now_2bit and not was_2bit:\\n                    n_2bit += 1\\n                elif was_2bit and not is_now_2bit:\\n                    n_2bit -= 1\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"

```text
1	"""Gemma-2-9B full pipeline: profile -> MCKP solve -> eval -> k ablation -> analysis.
2	
3	Gemma-2 adaptations vs Llama/Qwen baseline code:
4	  - head_dim from config (256), not hidden_size//num_heads (224)
5	  - attn_logit_softcapping: tanh(scores/softcap)*softcap before mask+softmax
6	  - query_pre_attn_scalar for attention scaling (not 1/sqrt(head_dim))
7	  - sliding_window on alternating layers (handled by 4D attention mask)
8	
9	Input:  model checkpoint, calibration JSON
10	Output: gemma2_full_results.json, gemma2_profiling_results.json
11	"""
12	import os
13	os.environ["HF_HOME"] = "/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache"
14	os.environ["HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE"] = "1"
15	
16	import argparse
17	import copy
18	import importlib
19	import json
20	import math
21	import time
22	import torch
23	import torch.nn.functional as F
24	from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, AutoConfig
25	from solver import solve_mckp_constrained, uniform_quantization_baseline, enforce_monotonicity
26	from utils import quantize_per_channel, quantize_per_token, repeat_kv
27	
28	
29	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
30	# Gemma-2-aware attention patching
31	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
32	
33	def _get_rotary_fn(model):
34	    model_type = model.config.model_type
35	    module_name = f"transformers.models.{model_type}.modeling_{model_type}"
36	    module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
37	    return module.apply_rotary_pos_emb
38	
39	
40	def patch_attention_gemma2(model, allocations):
41	    """Monkey-patch Gemma-2 attention with per-head quantization.
42	
43	    Handles head_dim != hidden_size//num_heads, logit soft-capping,
44	    and sliding window (via attention_mask from the model).
45	    """
46	    config = model.config
47	    num_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads
48	    num_q_heads = config.num_attention_heads
49	    head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", config.hidden_size // num_q_heads)
50	    n_groups = num_q_heads // num_kv_heads
51	    softcap = getattr(config, "attn_logit_softcapping", None)
52	
53	    rotary_fn = _get_rotary_fn(model)
54	    original_forwards = {}
55	
56	    for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(model.model.layers):
57	        attn = layer.self_attn
58	
59	        head_allocs = {}
60	        for h in range(num_kv_heads):
61	            key = f"{layer_idx},{h}"
62	            if key in allocations:
63	                atype, param = allocations[key]
64	                head_allocs[h] = (atype, param)
65	            else:
66	                head_allocs[h] = ("full", 16)
67	
68	        if all(a[0] == "full" for a in head_allocs.values()):
69	            continue
70	
71	        original_forwards[layer_idx] = attn.forward
72	
73	        def make_forward(orig_attn, h_allocs, n_kv=num_kv_heads, ng=n_groups,
74	                         hd=head_dim, rope_fn=rotary_fn, sc=softcap):
75	            def compressed_forward(hidden_states, position_embeddings,
76	                                   attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
77	                bsz, seq_len = hidden_states.shape[:2]
78	
79	                q = orig_attn.q_proj(hidden_states).view(bsz, seq_len, -1, hd).transpose(1, 2)
80	                k = orig_attn.k_proj(hidden_states).view(bsz, seq_len, n_kv, hd).transpose(1, 2)
81	                v = orig_attn.v_proj(hidden_states).view(bsz, seq_len, n_kv, hd).transpose(1, 2)
82	
83	                cos, sin = position_embeddings
84	                q, k = rope_fn(q, k, cos, sin)
85	
86	                for h in range(n_kv):
87	                    atype, param = h_allocs[h]
88	                    if atype == "quant" and int(param) < 16:
89	                        bits = int(param)
90	                        k[:, h] = quantize_per_channel(k[:, h:h + 1], bits).squeeze(1)
91	                        v[:, h] = quantize_per_token(v[:, h:h + 1], bits).squeeze(1)
92	
93	                k_exp = repeat_kv(k, ng)
94	                v_exp = repeat_kv(v, ng)
95	
96	                attn_scores = torch.matmul(q, k_exp.transpose(2, 3)) * orig_attn.scaling
97	
98	                # Gemma-2 logit soft-capping
99	                if sc is not None:
100	                    attn_scores = attn_scores / sc
101	                    attn_scores = torch.tanh(attn_scores)
102	                    attn_scores = attn_scores * sc
103	
104	                if attention_mask is not None:
105	                    cm = attention_mask[:, :, :, :k_exp.shape[-2]]
106	                    attn_scores = attn_scores + cm
107	                else:
108	                    causal = torch.triu(
109	                        torch.full((seq_len, seq_len), float("-inf"),
110	                                   device=attn_scores.device, dtype=attn_scores.dtype),
111	                        diagonal=1
112	                    )
113	                    attn_scores = attn_scores + causal
114	
115	                attn_w = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(q.dtype)
116	                out = torch.matmul(attn_w, v_exp)
117	                out = out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().reshape(bsz, seq_len, -1)
118	                out = orig_attn.o_proj(out)
119	                return out, attn_w
120	
121	            return compressed_forward
122	
123	        attn.forward = make_forward(attn, head_allocs)
124	
125	    return original_forwards
126	
127	
128	def restore_attention(model, original_forwards):
129	    for layer_idx, fwd in original_forwards.items():
130	        model.model.layers[layer_idx].self_attn.forward = fwd
131	
132	
133	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
134	# Profiler (replicates FullBitwidthProfiler logic with Gemma-2 patching)
135	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
136	
137	class Gemma2Profiler:
138	    def __init__(self, model, tokenizer, calibration_data, device="cuda:0",
139	                 num_samples=16, seq_len=512):
140	        self.device = device
141	        self.seq_len = seq_len
142	        self.model = model
143	        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
144	
145	        config = model.config
146	        self.num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers
147	        self.num_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads
148	
149	        self.input_ids = self._load_calibration_data(calibration_data)
150	        total_heads = self.num_layers * self.num_kv_heads
151	        print(f"Config: {self.num_layers} layers, {self.num_kv_heads} KV heads/layer, "
152	              f"{total_heads} heads total")
153	        print(f"Calibration: {self.input_ids.shape[0]} seqs x {self.input_ids.shape[1]} tokens")
154	        self._baseline_ppl = None
155	
156	    def _load_calibration_data(self, path):
157	        with open(path) as f:
158	            samples = json.load(f)
159	        all_ids = []
160	        for s in samples:
161	            tokens = self.tokenizer.encode(s["text"], add_special_tokens=False)
162	            if len(tokens) >= self.seq_len:
163	                all_ids.append(tokens[:self.seq_len])
164	            if len(all_ids) >= 16:
165	                break
166	        if not all_ids:
167	            raise RuntimeError(f"No calibration seqs with >= {self.seq_len} tokens")
168	        return torch.tensor(all_ids, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
169	
170	    def _compute_ppl(self):
171	        with torch.no_grad():
172	            outputs = self.model(
173	                self.input_ids, use_cache=False,
174	                cache_position=torch.arange(self.seq_len, device=self.device)
175	            )
176	        shift_logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1].contiguous()
177	        shift_labels = self.input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()
178	        loss = F.cross_entropy(
179	            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.shape[-1]),
180	            shift_labels.view(-1),
181	            reduction="sum"
182	        )
183	        return math.exp(loss.item() / shift_labels.numel())
184	
185	    def compute_baseline_ppl(self):
186	        if self._baseline_ppl is None:
187	            print("Computing baseline PPL...")
188	            self._baseline_ppl = self._compute_ppl()
189	            print(f"Baseline PPL: {self._baseline_ppl:.4f}")
190	        return self._baseline_ppl
191	
192	    def profile_single_head(self, layer, head, bits):
193	        self.compute_baseline_ppl()
194	        if bits >= 16:
195	            return 0.0
196	        allocations = {f"{layer},{head}": ["quant", bits]}
197	        orig_fwds = patch_attention_gemma2(self.model, allocations)
198	        ppl = self._compute_ppl()
199	        restore_attention(self.model, orig_fwds)
200	        return ppl - self._baseline_ppl
201	
202	    def profile_all(self, bit_widths=(2, 4, 8)):
203	        self.compute_baseline_ppl()
204	        total_heads = self.num_layers * self.num_kv_heads
205	        total_evals = total_heads * len(bit_widths)
206	        done = 0
207	
208	        profiles = {}
209	        for l in range(self.num_layers):
210	            profiles[str(l)] = {}
211	            for h in range(self.num_kv_heads):
212	                head_profile = {"quantization": {}}
213	                for bits in bit_widths:
214	                    delta = self.profile_single_head(l, h, bits)
215	                    head_profile["quantization"][str(bits)] = delta
216	                    done += 1
217	                    print(f"[{done}/{total_evals}] L{l}H{h} @{bits}bit: delta={delta:.6f}")
218	                profiles[str(l)][str(h)] = head_profile
219	        return profiles
220	
221	
222	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
223	# Eval (WikiText-2)
224	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
225	
226	def evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device, stride=512):
227	    from datasets import load_dataset, Dataset
228	    try:
229	        ds = load_dataset("wikitext", "wikitext-2-raw-v1", split="test")
230	    except Exception:
231	        arrow_path = "/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/datasets/wikitext/wikitext-2-raw-v1/0.0.0/b08601e04326c79dfdd32d625aee71d232d685c3/wikitext-test.arrow"
232	        print(f"  Hub unavailable, loading from arrow: {arrow_path}")
233	        ds = Dataset.from_file(arrow_path)
234	    text = "\n\n".join([t for t in ds["text"] if t.strip()])
235	
236	    encodings = tokenizer(text, return_tensors="pt")
237	    input_ids = encodings.input_ids.to(device)
238	    total_len = input_ids.shape[1]
239	    total_loss = 0.0
240	    total_tokens = 0
241	    n_chunks = (total_len - 1) // stride
242	
243	    print(f"Evaluating PPL on {total_len} tokens, stride={stride}, {n_chunks} chunks")
244	    for i, begin in enumerate(range(0, total_len - 1, stride)):
245	        end = min(begin + stride, total_len)
246	        chunk = input_ids[:, begin:end]
247	        with torch.no_grad():
248	            outputs = model(chunk, use_cache=False,
249	                            cache_position=torch.arange(chunk.shape[1], device=chunk.device))
250	        shift_logits = outputs.logits[:, :-1].contiguous()
251	        shift_labels = chunk[:, 1:].contiguous()
252	        loss = F.cross_entropy(
253	            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.shape[-1]),
254	            shift_labels.view(-1), reduction="sum")
255	        total_loss += loss.item()
256	        total_tokens += shift_labels.numel()
257	        if (i + 1) % 50 == 0:
258	            print(f"  chunk {i+1}/{n_chunks}, running ppl={math.exp(total_loss/total_tokens):.2f}")
259	
260	    return math.exp(total_loss / total_tokens)
261	
262	
263	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
264	# Main pipeline
265	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
266	
267	def main():
268	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Gemma-2-9B full pipeline")
269	    parser.add_argument("--model", type=str,
270	                        default="/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/google/gemma-2-9b")
271	    parser.add_argument("--calibration_data", type=str, default="calibration_data.json")
272	    parser.add_argument("--device", type=str, default="cuda:0")
273	    parser.add_argument("--budget", type=float, default=0.25)
274	    parser.add_argument("--initial_k", type=int, default=8)
275	    parser.add_argument("--k_values", type=str, default="2,4,6,8,10,12,14,16")
276	    parser.add_argument("--output", type=str, default="gemma2_full_results.json")
277	    parser.add_argument("--profile_output", type=str, default="gemma2_profiling_results.json")
278	    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
279	    args = parser.parse_args()
280	
281	    k_values = [int(x) for x in args.k_values.split(",")]
282	    device = args.device
283	
284	    # --- Verify model config ---
285	    print("=" * 60)
286	    print("Gemma-2-9B Full Pipeline")
287	    print("=" * 60)
288	
289	    config = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(args.model)
290	    num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers
291	    num_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads
292	    num_q_heads = config.num_attention_heads
293	    head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", config.hidden_size // num_q_heads)
294	    total_kv_heads = num_layers * num_kv_heads
295	    softcap = getattr(config, "attn_logit_softcapping", None)
296	    sliding_window = getattr(config, "sliding_window", None)
297	
298	    print(f"Model:         {args.model}")
299	    print(f"model_type:    {config.model_type}")
300	    print(f"Layers:        {num_layers}")
301	    print(f"Q heads:       {num_q_heads}")
302	    print(f"KV heads:      {num_kv_heads} (GQA {num_q_heads//num_kv_heads}:1)")
303	    print(f"head_dim:      {head_dim}")
304	    print(f"hidden_size:   {config.hidden_size}")
305	    print(f"Total KV heads:{total_kv_heads}")
306	    print(f"softcap:       {softcap}")
307	    print(f"sliding_window:{sliding_window}")
308	    print(f"Budget:        {args.budget}")
309	    print(f"Initial k:     {args.initial_k}")
310	    print(f"K sweep:       {k_values}")
311	
312	    if args.dry_run:
313	        print("\n[DRY RUN] Exiting.")
314	        return
315	
316	    # --- Load model ---
317	    print("\nLoading model...")
318	    t_load = time.time()
319	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model)
320	    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
321	        args.model, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation="eager"
322	    )
323	    model = model.to(device)
324	    model.eval()
325	    print(f"Model loaded in {time.time()-t_load:.1f}s")
326	
327	    # ===================================================================
328	    # Phase 1: Profiling
329	    # ===================================================================
330	    print(f"\n{'='*60}")
331	    print("[Phase 1] Profiling per-head PPL sensitivity...")
332	    print(f"{'='*60}")
333	    t0 = time.time()
334	
335	    profiler = Gemma2Profiler(
336	        model, tokenizer,
337	        calibration_data=args.calibration_data,
338	        device=device, num_samples=16, seq_len=512
339	    )
340	    baseline_cal_ppl = profiler.compute_baseline_ppl()
341	    profiles = profiler.profile_all(bit_widths=(2, 4, 8))
342	    profile_time = time.time() - t0
343	    print(f"\nProfiling done in {profile_time:.1f}s")
344	
345	    profile_data = {
346	        "model": args.model,
347	        "model_name": "Gemma-2-9B",
348	        "baseline_calibration_ppl": baseline_cal_ppl,
349	        "num_layers": num_layers,
350	        "num_kv_heads": num_kv_heads,
351	        "num_attention_heads": num_q_heads,
352	        "head_dim": head_dim,
353	        "gqa_ratio": num_q_heads // num_kv_heads,
354	        "profiles": profiles,
355	        "profile_time_seconds": profile_time,
356	    }
357	    with open(args.profile_output, "w") as f:
358	        json.dump(profile_data, f, indent=2)
359	    print(f"Profiles saved to {args.profile_output}")
360	
361	    _print_sensitivity(profiles)
362	
363	    # ===================================================================
364	    # Phase 2: Constrained MCKP + Uniform eval
365	    # ===================================================================
366	    print(f"\n{'='*60}")
367	    print(f"[Phase 2] Constrained MCKP (k={args.initial_k}) + Uniform 4-bit eval")
368	    print(f"{'='*60}")
369	    t1 = time.time()
370	
371	    enforced = enforce_monotonicity(profiles)
372	
373	    mckp_result = solve_mckp_constrained(
374	        enforced, args.budget, mode="quant_only",
375	        max_2bit=args.initial_k, l0_exclude_2bit=True
376	    )
377	    uniform_result = uniform_quantization_baseline(enforced, bits=4)
378	
379	    bit_counts = {}
380	    for key, (atype, param) in mckp_result["allocation"].items():
381	        b = param if atype == "quant" else 16
382	        bit_counts[b] = bit_counts.get(b, 0) + 1
383	    print(f"MCKP bit dist: {bit_counts}")
384	    print(f"MCKP mem ratio: {mckp_result['actual_memory_ratio']:.4f}")
385	
386	    # Evaluate bf16 baseline on WikiText-2
387	    print("\n  Evaluating bf16 baseline on WikiText-2...")
388	    baseline_wt2 = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device)

num_q_heads // num_kv_heads\n    total_kv_heads = num_layers * num_kv_heads\n\n    print(f\"Yi-1.5-9B: {num_layers} layers, {num_kv_heads} KV heads/layer, \"\n          f\"GQA {gqa_ratio}:1, {total_kv_heads} total KV heads\")\n    assert num_kv_heads == 4, f\"Expected 4 KV heads, got {num_kv_heads}\"\n    assert num_layers == 48, f\"Expected 48 layers, got {num_layers}\"\n\n    # ========== Phase 1: Profiling ==========\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE 1: Profiling (192 heads × 3 bitwidths)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    profile_start = time.time()\n    profiler = FullBitwidthProfiler(\n        model_path=args.model,\n        calibration_data=args.calibration_data,\n        device=device,\n        num_samples=16,\n        seq_len=512,\n    )\n    baseline_cal_ppl = profiler.compute_baseline_ppl()\n    profiles_raw = profiler.profile_all(bit_widths=(2, 4, 8))\n    profile_time = time.time() - profile_start\n\n    with open(\"yi15_profiling_results.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(profiles_raw, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"Phase 1 done in {profile_time / 60:.1f}min\")\n\n    _print_sensitivity(profiles_raw)\n\n    profiles = enforce_monotonicity(profiles_raw)\n\n    # ========== Phase 2: MCKP + Uniform + BF16 eval ==========\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"PHASE 2: MCKP (k={args.initial_k}) + Uniform + BF16 eval\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    phase2_start = time.time()\n    model = profiler.model\n    tokenizer = profiler.tokenizer\n\n    print(\"Evaluating bf16 baseline on WikiText-2...\")\n    baseline_wt2 = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device)\n    print(f\"BF16 WikiText-2 PPL: {baseline_wt2:.4f}\")\n\n    # MCKP allocation\n    mckp_result = solve_mckp_constrained(\n        profiles, args.budget, mode=\"quant_only\",\n        max_2bit=args.initial_k, l0_exclude_2bit=True)\n\n    bit_counts = {}\n    for key, (atype, param) in mckp_result[\"allocation\"].items():\n        b = int(param) if atype == \"quant\" else 16\n        bit_counts[b] = bit_counts.get(b, 0) + 1\n    print(f\"MCKP bit distribution: {bit_counts}\")\n\n    orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, mckp_result[\"allocation\"])\n    mckp_wt2 = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device)\n    restore_attention(model, orig_fwds)\n    print(f\"MCKP PPL: {mckp_wt2:.4f}\")\n\n    # Uniform 4-bit\n    uniform_result = uniform_quantization_baseline(profiles, bits=4)\n    orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, uniform_result[\"allocation\"])\n    uniform_wt2 = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device)\n    restore_attention(model, orig_fwds)\n    print(f\"Uniform 4-bit PPL: {uniform_wt2:.4f}\")\n\n    delta_phase2 = uniform_wt2 - mckp_wt2\n    print(f\"MCKP improvement over uniform: {delta_phase2:+.4f}\")\n    phase2_time = time.time() - phase2_start\n\n    # ========== Phase 3: K Ablation ==========\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE 3: K Ablation Sweep\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    ablation_start = time.time()\n    k_values = [2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16]\n    k_results = []\n\n    for k in k_values:\n        print(f\"\\n--- k={k} ---\")\n        result_k = solve_mckp_constrained(\n            profiles, args.budget, mode=\"quant_only\",\n            max_2bit=k, l0_exclude_2bit=True)\n\n        orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, result_k[\"allocation\"])\n        ppl_k = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device)\n        restore_attention(model, orig_fwds)\n\n        n_2bit = sum(1 for _, (a, p) in result_k[\"allocation\"].items()\n                     if a == \"quant\" and int(p) == 2)\n        k_entry = {\n            \"max_2bit\": k,\n            \"ppl\": round(ppl_k, 4),\n            \"n_2bit_actual\": n_2bit,\n            \"total_distortion\": result_k[\"total_distortion\"],\n            \"actual_memory_ratio\": result_k[\"actual_memory_ratio\"],\n        }\n        k_results.append(k_entry)\n        print(f\"k={k}: PPL={ppl_k:.4f}, n_2bit={n_2bit}\")\n\n    with open(\"yi15_k_ablation_results.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(k_results, f, indent=2)\n\n    best_k = min(k_results, key=lambda x: x[\"ppl\"])\n    print(f\"\\nBest k={best_k['max_2bit']} with PPL={best_k['ppl']:.4f}\")\n    ablation_time = time.time() - ablation_start\n\n    # ========== Phase 4: Analysis ==========\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE 4: Heterogeneity Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    all_deltas_2bit = []\n    layer_deltas = {}\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=int):\n        l = int(l_str)\n        layer_deltas[l] = []\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=int):\n            d2 = profiles[l_str][h_str][\"quantization\"].get(\"2\", 0.0)\n            all_deltas_2bit.append((l, int(h_str), d2))\n            layer_deltas[l].append(d2)\n\n    # Top-5% concentration\n    sorted_by_d2 = sorted(all_deltas_2bit, key=lambda x: -x[2])\n    n_top5 = max(1, int(len(sorted_by_d2) * 0.05))\n    total_sum = sum(x[2] for x in all_deltas_2bit)\n    top5_sum = sum(x[2] for x in sorted_by_d2[:n_top5])\n    top5_concentration = top5_sum / total_sum if total_sum > 0 else 0\n\n    # Layer 0 dominance\n    l0_deltas = layer_deltas.get(0, [0])\n    l0_mean = sum(l0_deltas) / max(len(l0_deltas), 1)\n    other_means = []\n    for l in range(1, num_layers):\n        if l in layer_deltas and layer_deltas[l]:\n            other_means.append(sum(layer_deltas[l]) / len(layer_deltas[l]))\n    other_avg = sum(other_means) / len(other_means) if other_means else 0\n    l0_ratio = l0_mean / other_avg if other_avg > 0 else float(\"inf\")\n\n    # General stats\n    d_tensor = torch.tensor([x[2] for x in all_deltas_2bit])\n    n_positive = int((d_tensor > 1e-6).sum().item())\n    cv = d_tensor.std().item() / d_tensor.mean().item() if d_tensor.mean().item() > 0 else 0\n\n    print(f\"Top-5% concentration: {top5_concentration:.4f} ({n_top5} heads)\")\n    print(f\"Layer 0 mean delta(2bit): {l0_mean:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Other layers mean delta(2bit): {other_avg:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"L0 dominance ratio: {l0_ratio:.2f}x\")\n    print(f\"Heads with positive delta: {n_positive}/{len(all_deltas_2bit)}\")\n    print(f\"CV of delta(2bit): {cv:.4f}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nTop-5 most sensitive heads:\")\n    for name_l, name_h, d2 in sorted_by_d2[:5]:\n        d4 = profiles[str(name_l)][str(name_h)][\"quantization\"].get(\"4\", 0.0)\n        d8 = profiles[str(name_l)][str(name_h)][\"quantization\"].get(\"8\", 0.0)\n        print(f\"  L{name_l}H{name_h}: 2bit={d2:+.4f}, 4bit={d4:+.4f}, 8bit={d8:+.4f}\")\n\n    # Solver equivalence check (greedy vs MCKP should match)\n    from solver import solve_ratequant_greedy\n    greedy_result = solve_ratequant_greedy(profiles, args.budget, mode=\"quant_only\")\n    greedy_dist = greedy_result[\"total_distortion\"]\n    mckp_dist = mckp_result[\"total_distortion\"]\n    print(f\"\\nSolver equivalence: greedy={greedy_dist:.6f}, mckp={mckp_dist:.6f}, \"\n          f\"delta={abs(greedy_dist - mckp_dist):.6f}\")\n\n    # ========== Phase 5: Save Results ==========\n    total_time = time.time() - t0\n\n    results = {\n        \"model\": args.model,\n        \"model_name\": \"Yi-1.5-9B\",\n        \"model_type\": config.model_type,\n        \"architecture\": {\n            \"num_layers\": num_layers,\n            \"num_kv_heads\": num_kv_heads,\n            \"num_attention_heads\": num_q_heads,\n            \"head_dim\": config.hidden_size // num_q_heads,\n            \"hidden_size\": config.hidden_size,\n            \"gqa_ratio\": gqa_ratio,\n            \"total_kv_heads\": total_kv_heads,\n        },\n        \"verdict\": \"PASS\" if delta_phase2 >= 0.5 else \"MARGINAL\" if delta_phase2 >= 0.1 else \"FAIL\",\n        \"baseline_calibration_ppl\": baseline_cal_ppl,\n        \"baseline_wikitext2_ppl\": baseline_wt2,\n        \"mckp_wikitext2_ppl\": mckp_wt2,\n        \"uniform4bit_wikitext2_ppl\": uniform_wt2,\n        \"mckp_improvement_over_uniform\": delta_phase2,\n        \"initial_k\": args.initial_k,\n        \"bit_distribution\": bit_counts,\n        \"mckp_actual_memory_ratio\": mckp_result[\"actual_memory_ratio\"],\n        \"k_ablation\": k_results,\n        \"best_k\": best_k,\n        \"analysis\": {\n            \"top5_concentration\": round(top5_concentration, 4),\n            \"top5_n_heads\": n_top5,\n            \"layer0_mean_delta_2bit\": round(l0_mean, 6),\n            \"other_layers_mean_delta_2bit\": round(other_avg, 6),\n            \"layer0_dominance_ratio\": round(l0_ratio, 2),\n            \"delta_2bit_mean\": round(d_tensor.mean().item(), 6),\n            \"delta_2bit_std\": round(d_tensor.std().item(), 6),\n            \"delta_2bit_cv\": round(cv, 4),\n            \"heads_with_positive_delta\": n_positive,\n        },\n        \"timing\": {\n            \"profile_seconds\": round(profile_time, 1),\n            \"phase2_seconds\": round(phase2_time, 1),\n            \"ablation_seconds\": round(ablation_time, 1),\n            \"total_seconds\": round(total_time, 1),\n        },\n        \"config\": {\n            \"budget\": args.budget,\n            \"initial_k\": args.initial_k,\n            \"k_values\": k_values,\n            \"calibration_data\": args.calibration_data,\n        },\n    }\n\n    with open(args.output, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 60}\")\n    print(\"RESULTS — Yi-1.5-9B\")\n    print(f\"{'=' * 60}\")\n    print(f\"\\n{'Method':<30} {'PPL':>10} {'Delta vs uniform':>18}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n    print(f\"{'Baseline (bf16)':<30} {baseline_wt2:>10.4f} {'---':>18}\")\n    print(f\"{'Non-uniform MCKP':<30} {mckp_wt2:>10.4f} {delta_phase2:>+18.4f}\")\n    print(f\"{'Uniform 4-bit':<30} {uniform_wt2:>10.4f} {'---':>18}\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop-5% concentration: {top5_concentration:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"L0 dominance: {l0_ratio:.2f}x\")\n    print(f\"Best k: {best_k['max_2bit']} (PPL={best_k['ppl']:.4f})\")\n    print(f\"Verdict: {results['verdict']}\")\n    print(f\"Total time: {total_time / 3600:.1f}h\")\n    print(f\"\\nSaved to {args.output}\")\n\n\ndef _print_sensitivity(profiles):\n    all_2bit = []\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=int):\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=int):\n            delta_2 = profiles[l_str][h_str][\"quantization\"].get(\"2\", 0.0)\n            delta_4 = profiles[l_str][h_str][\"quantization\"].get(\"4\", 0.0)\n            delta_8 = profiles[l_str][h_str][\"quantization\"].get(\"8\", 0.0)\n            all_2bit.append((f\"L{l_str}H{h_str}\", delta_2, delta_4, delta_8))\n\n    deltas = [x[1] for x in all_2bit]\n    print(f\"\\n2-bit delta stats: min={min(deltas):.4f}, max={max(deltas):.4f}, \"\n          f\"mean={sum(deltas) / len(deltas):.4f}, \"\n          f\"std={torch.tensor(deltas).std().item():.4f}\")\n\n    by_2bit = sorted(all_2bit, key=lambda x: -x[1])\n    print(\"\\nTop-5 most sensitive heads (2-bit):\")\n    for name, d2, d4, d8 in by_2bit[:5]:\n        print(f\"  {name}: 2bit={d2:+.4f}, 4bit={d4:+.4f}, 8bit={d8:+.4f}\")\n    print(\"\\nBottom-5 least sensitive heads (2-bit):\")\n    for name, d2, d4, d8 in by_2bit[-5:]:\n        print(f\"  {name}: 2bit={d2:+.4f}, 4bit={d4:+.4f}, os\nos.environ[\\\"HF_HOME\\\"] = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\\"\nos.environ[\\\"HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE\\\"] = \\\"1\\\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport math\nimport time\nimport torch\nfrom required=True)\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--calibration_data\\\", type=str, default=\\\"calibration_data.json\\\")\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, default=\\\"yi15_full_results.json\\\")\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--budget\\\", type=float, default=0.25)\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--initial_k\\\", type=int, default=8)\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--gpu\\\", type=str, default=\\\"cuda:1\\\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    device = args.gpu\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    config = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(args.model)\n    num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\n    num_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads\n    num_q_heads = config.num_attention_heads\n    gqa_ratio = num_q_heads // num_kv_heads\n    total_kv_heads = num_layers * num_kv_heads\n\n    print(f\\\"Yi-1.5-9B: {num_layers} layers, {num_kv_heads} KV heads/layer, \\\"\n          f\\\"GQA {gqa_ratio}:1, {total_kv_heads} total KV heads\\\")\n    assert num_kv_heads == 4, f\\\"Expected 4 KV heads, got {num_kv_heads}\\\"\n    assert num_layers == 48, f\\\"Expected 48 layers, got {num_layers}\\\"\n\n    # ========== Phase 1: Profiling ==========\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 60)\n    print(\\\"PHASE 1: Profiling (192 heads x 3 bitwidths)\\\")\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\n\n    profile_start = time.time()\n    profiler = FullBitwidthProfiler(\n        model_path=args.model,\n        calibration_data=args.calibration_data,\n        device=device,\n        num_samples=16,\n        seq_len=512,\n    )\n    baseline_cal_ppl = profiler.compute_baseline_ppl()\n    profiles_raw = profiler.profile_all(bit_widths=(2, 4, 8))\n    profile_time = time.time() - profile_start\n\n    with open(\\\"yi15_profiling_results.json\\\", \\\"w\\\") as f:\n        json.dump(profiles_raw, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\\\"Phase 1 done in {profile_time / 60:.1f}min\\\")\n\n    _print_sensitivity(profiles_raw)\n\n    profiles = enforce_monotonicity(profiles_raw)\n\n    # ========== Phase 2: MCKP + Uniform + BF16 eval ==========\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 60)\n    print(f\\\"PHASE 2: MCKP (k={args.initial_k}) + Uniform + BF16 eval\\\")\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\n\n    phase2_start = time.time()\n    model = profiler.model\n    tokenizer = profiler.tokenizer\n\n    print(\\\"Evaluating bf16 baseline on WikiText-2...\\\")\n    baseline_wt2 = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device)\n    print(f\\\"BF16 WikiText-2 PPL: {baseline_wt2:.4f}\\\")\n\n    mckp_result = solve_mckp_constrained(\n        profiles, args.budget, mode=\\\"quant_only\\\",\n        max_2bit=args.initial_k, l0_exclude_2bit=True)\n\n    bit_counts = {}\n    for key, (atype, param) in mckp_result[\\\"allocation\\\"].items():\n        b = int(param) if atype == \\\"quant\\\" else 16\n        bit_counts[b] = bit_counts.get(b, 0) + 1\n    print(f\\\"MCKP bit distribution: {bit_counts}\\\")\n\n    orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, mckp_result[\\\"allocation\\\"])\n    mckp_wt2 = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device)\n    restore_attention(model, orig_fwds)\n    print(f\\\"MCKP PPL: {mckp_wt2:.4f}\\\")\n\n    uniform_result = uniform_quantization_baseline(profiles, bits=4)\n    orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, uniform_result[\\\"allocation\\\"])\n    uniform_wt2 = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device)\n    restore_attention(model, orig_fwds)\n    print(f\\\"Uniform 4-bit PPL: {uniform_wt2:.4f}\\\")\n\n    delta_phase2 = uniform_wt2 - mckp_wt2\n    print(f\\\"MCKP improvement over uniform: {delta_phase2:+.4f}\\\")\n    phase2_time = time.time() - phase2_start\n\n    # ========== Phase 3: K Ablation ==========\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 60)\n    print(\\\"PHASE 3: K Ablation Sweep\\\")\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\n\n    ablation_start = time.time()\n    k_values = [2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16]\n    k_results = []\n\n    for k in k_values:\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n--- k={k} ---\\\")\n        result_k = solve_mckp_constrained(\n            profiles, args.budget, mode=\\\"quant_only\\\",\n            max_2bit=k, l0_exclude_2bit=True)\n\n        orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, result_k[\\\"allocation\\\"])\n        ppl_k = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device)\n        restore_attention(model, orig_fwds)\n\n        n_2bit = sum(1 for _, (a, p) in result_k[\\\"allocation\\\"].items()\n                     if a == \\\"quant\\\" and int(p) == 2)\n        k_entry = {\n            \\\"max_2bit\\\": k,\n            \\\"ppl\\\": round(ppl_k, 4),\n            \\\"n_2bit_actual\\\": n_2bit,\n            \\\"total_distortion\\\": result_k[\\\"total_distortion\\\"],\n            \\\"actual_memory_ratio\\\": result_k[\\\"actual_memory_ratio\\\"],\n        }\n        k_results.append(k_entry)\n        print(f\\\"k={k}: PPL={ppl_k:.4f}, n_2bit={n_2bit}\\\")\n\n    with open(\\\"yi15_k_ablation_results.json\\\", \\\"w\\\") as f:\n        json.dump(k_results, f, indent=2)\n\n    best_k = min(k_results, key=lambda x: x[\\\"ppl\\\"])\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nBest k={best_k['max_2bit']} with PPL={best_k['ppl']:.4f}\\\")\n    ablation_time = time.time() - ablation_start\n\n    # ========== Phase 4: Analysis ==========\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 60)\n    print(\\\"PHASE 4: Heterogeneity Analysis\\\")\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\n\n    all_deltas_2bit = []\n    layer_deltas = {}\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=int):\n        l = int(l_str)\n        layer_deltas[l] = []\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=int):\n            d2 = profiles[l_str][h_str][\\\"quantization\\\"].get(\\\"2\\\", 0.0)\n            all_deltas_2bit.append((l, int(h_str), d2))\n            layer_deltas[l].append(d2)\n\n    sorted_by_d2 = sorted(all_deltas_2bit, key=lambda x: -x[2])\n    n_top5 = max(1, int(len(sorted_by_d2) * 0.05))\n    total_sum = sum(x[2] for x in all_deltas_2bit)\n    top5_sum = sum(x[2] for x in sorted_by_d2[:n_top5])\n    top5_concentration = top5_sum / total_sum if total_sum > 0 else 0\n\n    l0_deltas = layer_deltas.get(0, [0])\n    l0_mean = sum(l0_deltas) / max(len(l0_deltas), 1)\n    other_means = []\n    for l in range(1, num_layers):\n        if l in layer_deltas and layer_deltas[l]:\n            other_means.append(sum(layer_deltas[l]) / len(layer_deltas[l]))\n    other_avg = sum(other_means) / len(other_means) if other_means else 0\n    l0_ratio = l0_mean / other_avg if other_avg > 0 else float(\\\"inf\\\")\n\n    d_tensor = torch.tensor([x[2] for x in all_deltas_2bit])\n    n_positive = int((d_tensor > 1e-6).sum().item())\n    cv = d_tensor.std().item() / d_tensor.mean().item() if d_tensor.mean().item() > 0 else 0\n\n    print(f\\\"Top-5% concentration: {top5_concentration:.4f} ({n_top5} heads)\\\")\n    print(f\\\"Layer 0 mean delta(2bit): {l0_mean:.6f}\\\")\n    print(f\\\"Other layers mean delta(2bit): {other_avg:.6f}\\\")\n    print(f\\\"L0 dominance ratio: {l0_ratio:.2f}x\\\")\n    print(f\\\"Heads with positive delta: {n_positive}/{len(all_deltas_2bit)}\\\")\n    print(f\\\"CV of delta(2bit): {cv:.4f}\\\")\n\n    print(\\\"\\\\nTop-5 most sensitive heads:\\\")\n    for name_l, name_h, d2 in sorted_by_d2[:5]:\n        d4 = profiles[str(name_l)][str(name_h)][\\\"quantization\\\"].get(\\\"4\\\", 0.0)\n        d8 = profiles[str(name_l)][str(name_h)][\\\"quantization\\\"].get(\\\"8\\\", 0.0)\n        print(f\\\"  L{name_l}H{name_h}: 2bit={d2:+.4f}, 4bit={d4:+.4f}, 8bit={d8:+.4f}\\\")\n\n    from solver import solve_ratequant_greedy\n    greedy_result = solve_ratequant_greedy(profiles, args.budget, mode=\\\"quant_only\\\")\n    greedy_dist = greedy_result[\\\"total_distortion\\\"]\n    mckp_dist = mckp_result[\\\"total_distortion\\\"]\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nSolver equivalence: greedy={greedy_dist:.6f}, mckp={mckp_dist:.6f}, \\\"\n          f\\\"delta={abs(greedy_dist - mckp_dist):.6f}\\\")\n\n    # ========== Phase 5: Save Results ==========\n    total_time = time.time() - t0\n\n    results = {\n        \\\"model\\\": args.model,\n        \\\"model_name\\\": \\\"Yi-1.5-9B\\\",\n        \\\"model_type\\\": config.model_type,\n        \\\"architecture\\\": {\n            \\\"num_layers\\\": num_layers,\n            \\\"num_kv_heads\\\": num_kv_heads,\n            \\\"num_attention_heads\\\": num_q_heads,\n            \\\"head_dim\\\": config.hidden_size // num_q_heads,\n            \\\"hidden_size\\\": config.hidden_size,\n            \\\"gqa_ratio\\\": gqa_ratio,\n            \\\"total_kv_heads\\\": total_kv_heads,\n        },\n        \\\"verdict\\\": \\\"PASS\\\" if delta_phase2 >= 0.5 else \\\"MARGINAL\\\" if delta_phase2 >= 0.1 else \\\"FAIL\\\",\n        \\\"baseline_calibration_ppl\\\": baseline_cal_ppl,\n        \\\"baseline_wikitext2_ppl\\\": baseline_wt2,\n        \\\"mckp_wikitext2_ppl\\\": mckp_wt2,\n        \\\"uniform4bit_wikitext2_ppl\\\": uniform_wt2,\n        \\\"mckp_improvement_over_uniform\\\": delta_phase2,\n        \\\"initial_k\\\": args.initial_k,\n        \\\"bit_distribution\\\": bit_counts,\n        \\\"mckp_actual_memory_ratio\\\": mckp_result[\\\"actual_memory_ratio\\\"],\n        \\\"k_ablation\\\": k_results,\n        \\\"best_k\\\": best_k,\n        \\\"analysis\\\": {\n            \\\"top5_concentration\\\": round(top5_concentration, 4),\n            \\\"top5_n_heads\\\": n_top5,\n            \\\"layer0_mean_delta_2bit\\\": round(l0_mean, 6),\n            \\\"other_layers_mean_delta_2bit\\\": round(other_avg, 6),\n            \\\"layer0_dominance_ratio\\\": round(l0_ratio, 2),\n            \\\"delta_2bit_mean\\\": round(d_tensor.mean().item(), 6),\n            \\\"delta_2bit_std\\\": round(d_tensor.std().item(), 6),\n            \\\"delta_2bit_cv\\\": round(cv, 4),\n            \\\"heads_with_positive_delta\\\": n_positive,\n        },\n        \\\"timing\\\": {\n            \\\"profile_seconds\\\": round(profile_time, 1),\n            \\\"phase2_seconds\\\": round(phase2_time, 1),\n            \\\"ablation_seconds\\\": round(ablation_time, 1),\n            \\\"total_seconds\\\": round(total_time, 1),\n        },\n        \\\"config\\\": {\n            \\\"budget\\\": args.budget,\n            \\\"initial_k\\\": args.initial_k,\n            \\\"k_values\\\": k_values,\n            \\\"calibration_data\\\": args.calibration_data,\n        },\n    }\n\n    with open(args.output, \\\"w\\\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n{'=' * 60}\\\")\n    print(\\\"RESULTS -- Yi-1.5-9B\\\")\n    print(f\\\"{'=' * 60}\\\")\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n{'Method':<30} {'PPL':>10} {'Delta vs uniform':>18}\\\")\n    print(\\\"-\\\" * 60)\n    print(f\\\"{'Baseline (bf16)':<30} {baseline_wt2:>10.4f} {'---':>18}\\\")\n    print(f\\\"{'Non-uniform MCKP':<30} {mckp_wt2:>10.4f} {delta_phase2:>+18.4f}\\\")\n    print(f\\\"{'Uniform 4-bit':<30} {uniform_wt2:>10.4f} {'---':>18}\\\")\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nTop-5% concentration: {top5_concentration:.4f}\\\")\n    print(f\\\"L0 dominance: {l0_ratio:.2f}x\\\")\n    print(f\\\"Best k: {best_k['max_2bit']} (PPL={best_k['ppl']:.4f})\\\")\n    print(f\\\"Verdict: {results['verdict']}\\\")\n    print(f\\\"Total time: {total_time / 3600:.1f}h\\\")\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nSaved to {args.output}\\\")\n\n\ndef _print_sensitivity(profiles):\n    all_2bit = []\n    for l_str in sorted(profiles.keys(), key=int):\n        for h_str in sorted(profiles[l_str].keys(), key=int):\n            delta_2 = profiles[l_str][h_str][\\\"quantization\\\"].get(\\\"2\\\", 0.0)\n            delta_4 = profiles[l_str][h_str][\\\"quantization\\\"].get(\\\"4\\\", 0.0)\n            delta_8 = profiles[l_str][h_str][\\\"quantization\\\"].get(\\\"8\\\", 0.0)\n            all_2bit.append((f\\\"L{l_str}H{h_str}\\\", delta_2, delta_4, delta_8))\n\n    deltas = [x[1] for x in all_2bit]\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n2-bit delta stats: min={min(deltas):.4f}, max={max(deltas):.4f}, \\\"\n          f\\\"mean={sum(deltas) / len(deltas):.4f}, \\\"\n          f\\\"std={torch.tensor(deltas).std().item():.4f}\\\")\n\n    by_2bit = sorted(all_2bit, key=lambda x: -x[1])\n    print(\\\"\\\\nTop-5 most sensitive heads (2-bit):\\\")\n    for name, d2, d4, d8 in by_2bit[:5]:\n        print(f\\\"  {name}: 2bit={d2:+.4f}, 4bit={d4:+.4f}, 8bit={d8:+.4f}\\\")\n    print(\\\"\\\\nBottom-5 least sensitive heads (2-bit):\\\")\n    for name, d2, d4, d8 in by_2bit[-5:]:\n        print(f\\\"  {name}: 2bit={d2:+.4f}, 4bit={d4:+.4f}, 8bit={d8:+.4f}\\\")\n\n\nif
